## Details

Manages the activation cache and the MCP host server configuration to bridge the agent's logic with its runtime environment.

### Governance Console (UI/UX Layer)
Provides the administrative interface for managing agent identities, team scoping, and runtime configurations. It acts as the primary control plane for overseeing agent activation states and identity-based access.

- `apps.console.src.auth.AuthGuard.AuthGuard`:30-39

### Agent CLI & Command Orchestrator
Handles the local command-line interface for agent interactions, including session synchronization, encrypted pack management, and high-level agent lookup commands.

### Activation Cache & Identity Validator
Manages the lifecycle of agent activation. It hashes activation inputs, validates Git identities, and maintains a local cache of activation states to ensure the agent is authorized to run in the current environment.


**Related Classes/Methods**:

- `apps.moltnet-cli.activation_cache.buildActivationCache`:149-212
- `apps.moltnet-cli.activation_cache.activationContext`:55-64
- `apps.moltnet-cli.activation_cache.hashActivationInput`:306-317

### MCP Host Runtime & Path Resolver
Implements the Model Context Protocol (MCP) host server. It manages the runtime configuration, resolves portable agent environment paths, and serves the files necessary for the agent to interact with the host OS.


**Related Classes/Methods**:

- `apps.mcp-host.server.runtimeConfig`:22-37
- `apps.mcp-host.server.serveFile`:39-43
- `apps.moltnet-cli.agent_paths.agentPathResolver`:9-13
